\begin{isamarkuptext}%
The accumulator fold stays inside \isa{{\isasymGamma}\ {\isasymunion}\ {\isacharbraceleft}{\kern0pt}bl{\isacharbraceright}{\kern0pt}}:
  on a non-empty bit list the outermost \isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}acc} is a
  \isa{gamma{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}unenum} image (always in range by \isa{gamma{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}unenum{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}mem});
  on the empty list it is the seed.  This is the read-loop's
  \isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}valid{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}stage} obligation discharged once for the running
  \isa{buf} value, independent of how many bits have been folded so
  far.
  Throughout, \isa{b} abbreviates \isa{block{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}width\ {\isasymGamma}} (the per-symbol cell width).%
\end{isamarkuptext}\isamarkuptrue%

\ foldl{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}acc{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}mem{\isacharcolon}{\kern0pt}\isanewline

\begin{isamarkuptext}%
The inner read loop, single tape, proper cell: starting at
  bit-counter \isa{{\isadigit{2}}} with the partial decode \isa{buf{\isadigit{0}}} and the
  head at \isa{base} (the first bit cell, \isa{sim{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}pos\ p}), the
  \isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}read{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}bit{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}step} arm fires \isa{b\ {\isacharminus}{\kern0pt}\ {\isadigit{1}}} times, walking
  \isa{R} across the first \isa{b\ {\isacharminus}{\kern0pt}\ {\isadigit{1}}} bit cells and folding each
  into \isa{buf\ tk} via \isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}acc}.  After the loop the counter
  is \isa{Suc\ b} (ready for the boundary substep that reads the
  \isa{b}-th, last bit), the head is at \isa{base\ {\isacharplus}{\kern0pt}\ {\isacharparenleft}{\kern0pt}b\ {\isacharminus}{\kern0pt}\ {\isadigit{1}}{\isacharparenright}{\kern0pt}}, the
  tape is untouched, and every other tape's head is where it was.
  Instantiates \isa{relpow{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}invariant{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}chain}
  with the loop-index-parameterised invariant whose \isa{buf\ tk}
  field is the \isa{foldl\ {\isacharparenleft}{\kern0pt}ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}acc{\isacharparenright}{\kern0pt}\ buf{\isadigit{0}}{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}tk} of the cells read so
  far — the same \isa{foldl} the read-decode arithmetic
  (\isa{foldl{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}acc{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}encode{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}symbol}) is stated against, so the
  per-step buf update is one \isa{foldl{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}append} rewrite.%
\end{isamarkuptext}\isamarkuptrue%
%
\begin{isamarkuptext}%
Relation-generic read bit-accumulation loop scaffold.  The
  bit-reading leaf (\isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}read{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}bit{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}step}) is the \isa{leaf}
  hypothesis, quantified over config, counter, and buffer (the
  accumulator mutates each step); union and sub versions are thin
  instantiations.%
\end{isamarkuptext}\isamarkuptrue%

\begin{isamarkuptext}%
The proper-cell single-tape read, non-last tape.  From an
  \isa{AR{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}SimRead} stage at bit-counter \isa{{\isadigit{0}}} with the head over a
  proper block \isa{base\ {\isacharequal}{\kern0pt}\ sim{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}pos\ p} (\isa{p\ {\isasymge}\ {\isadigit{1}}}, so
  \isa{{\isadigit{0}}\ {\isacharless}{\kern0pt}\ base}) whose current cell is not \isa{LE{\isadigit{4}}} and \isa{posk\ tk} proper, the read fires the two look-back steps, the inner
  per-bit loop (\isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}read{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}bit{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}loop}), and the bit boundary,
  decoding the \isa{b}-cell block at \isa{base} into \isa{buf\ tk} and landing at the next tape's \isa{AR{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}SimRead}
  (\isa{k{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}succ\ tk}, counter \isa{{\isadigit{0}}}).  The decoded \isa{buf\ tk} is the abstract fold \isa{foldl\ {\isacharparenleft}{\kern0pt}ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}acc\ {\isasymGamma}\ bl{\isacharparenright}{\kern0pt}\ {\isacharparenleft}{\kern0pt}gamma{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}unenum\ {\isasymGamma}\ bl\ {\isadigit{0}}{\isacharparenright}{\kern0pt}} over the block's \isa{b} cells --
  the form the caller collapses to the source symbol via
  \isa{foldl{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}acc{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}cell{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}repr} once tape-correspondence pins those
  cells to \isa{cell{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}repr}.  The chain has length \isa{b\ {\isacharplus}{\kern0pt}\ {\isadigit{2}}}
  (look-back 1, look-back 2, loop \isa{b\ {\isacharminus}{\kern0pt}\ {\isadigit{1}}}, boundary).  The
  refined \isa{posk\ tk} records whether the look-back cell was
  \isa{LE{\isadigit{4}}} (the head sat at \isa{sim{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}pos\ {\isadigit{1}}}).%
\end{isamarkuptext}\isamarkuptrue%
%
\begin{isamarkuptext}%
The snoc step of a left fold over a \isa{map\ f\ {\isacharbrackleft}{\kern0pt}{\isadigit{0}}{\isachardot}{\kern0pt}{\isachardot}{\kern0pt}{\isacharless}{\kern0pt}b{\isacharbrackright}{\kern0pt}}:
  for \isa{{\isadigit{0}}\ {\isacharless}{\kern0pt}\ b} the fold equals one \isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}acc} applied to the
  fold over the first \isa{b\ {\isacharminus}{\kern0pt}\ {\isadigit{1}}} cells and the last cell
  \isa{f\ {\isacharparenleft}{\kern0pt}b\ {\isacharminus}{\kern0pt}\ {\isadigit{1}}{\isacharparenright}{\kern0pt}}.  This is the boundary/finish step's last-bit
  accumulator collapse, shared by both proper-read consumers.%
\end{isamarkuptext}\isamarkuptrue%

\begin{isamarkuptext}%
The proper-cell read prefix: the part of a proper-cell
  single-tape read shared by the non-last (\isa{boundary}) and last
  (\isa{finish}) variants.  From an \isa{AR{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}SimRead} stage at
  bit-counter \isa{{\isadigit{0}}} with the head over a proper block
  \isa{base\ {\isacharequal}{\kern0pt}\ sim{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}pos\ p} (\isa{{\isadigit{0}}\ {\isacharless}{\kern0pt}\ base}) whose cell is not
  \isa{LE{\isadigit{4}}} and \isa{posk\ tk} proper, it fires look-back 1, look-back
  2, and the inner per-bit loop (\isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}read{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}bit{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}loop}), reaching
  bit-counter \isa{Suc\ b} with the head at \isa{base\ {\isacharplus}{\kern0pt}\ {\isacharparenleft}{\kern0pt}b\ {\isacharminus}{\kern0pt}\ {\isadigit{1}}{\isacharparenright}{\kern0pt}} and
  \isa{buf\ tk} the fold over the first \isa{b\ {\isacharminus}{\kern0pt}\ {\isadigit{1}}} cells.  The
  remaining last-bit step (boundary or finish) is added by the
  consumer.  Chain length \isa{Suc\ b} (\isa{{\isadigit{1}}\ {\isacharplus}{\kern0pt}\ {\isadigit{1}}\ {\isacharplus}{\kern0pt}\ {\isacharparenleft}{\kern0pt}b\ {\isacharminus}{\kern0pt}\ {\isadigit{1}}{\isacharparenright}{\kern0pt}}).%
\end{isamarkuptext}\isamarkuptrue%
%
\begin{isamarkuptext}%
Relation-generic read proper-prefix scaffold.  The two
  lookback steps and the bit loop are the \isa{leaf{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}lb{\isadigit{1}}},
  \isa{leaf{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}lb{\isadigit{2}}}, \isa{leaf{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}loop} hypotheses; union and sub versions
  are thin instantiations.%
\end{isamarkuptext}\isamarkuptrue%

\begin{isamarkuptext}%
The proper-cell single-tape read, non-last tape: the prefix
  followed by the bit-boundary step (\isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}read{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}bit{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}boundary{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}step}),
  decoding the \isa{b}-cell block at \isa{base} into \isa{buf\ tk}
  (the abstract fold \isa{foldl\ {\isacharparenleft}{\kern0pt}ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}acc\ {\isasymGamma}\ bl{\isacharparenright}{\kern0pt}\ {\isacharparenleft}{\kern0pt}gamma{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}unenum\ {\isasymGamma}\ bl\ {\isadigit{0}}{\isacharparenright}{\kern0pt}}, collapsed to the source symbol by
  \isa{foldl{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}acc{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}cell{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}repr}) and landing at the next tape's
  \isa{AR{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}SimRead} (\isa{k{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}succ\ tk}, counter \isa{{\isadigit{0}}}).  Chain length
  \isa{b\ {\isacharplus}{\kern0pt}\ {\isadigit{2}}}.%
\end{isamarkuptext}\isamarkuptrue%
%
\begin{isamarkuptext}%
Relation-generic read proper-step scaffold.  The prefix walk
  and the closing bit-boundary step are the \isa{leaf{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}prefix} and
  \isa{leaf{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}boundary} hypotheses; union and sub versions are thin
  instantiations.%
\end{isamarkuptext}\isamarkuptrue%

\begin{isamarkuptext}%
The proper-cell single-tape read, last tape: the prefix
  followed by the bit-finish step (\isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}read{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}bit{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}finish{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}step}),
  decoding the \isa{b}-cell block at \isa{base} into \isa{buf\ tk}
  exactly as the non-last variant, but transitioning to
  \isa{AR{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}SimCompute} with the current-tape field reset to
  \isa{k{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}unidx\ {\isadigit{0}}} (all tapes decoded).  Chain length \isa{b\ {\isacharplus}{\kern0pt}\ {\isadigit{2}}}.%
\end{isamarkuptext}\isamarkuptrue%
%
\begin{isamarkuptext}%
Relation-generic read proper-finish-step scaffold.  As
  \isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}read{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}proper{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}step{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}gen} but the closing leaf is the last-tape
  bit-finish (transition to \isa{AR{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}SimCompute}); the prefix and
  finish helpers are the \isa{leaf{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}prefix} and \isa{leaf{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}finish}
  hypotheses.%
\end{isamarkuptext}\isamarkuptrue%

\begin{isamarkuptext}%
Unified single-tape read, non-last tape: dispatches the per-tape
  read on whether \isa{M}'s head sits on the left-end marker
  (\isa{p\ {\isacharequal}{\kern0pt}\ {\isadigit{0}}}, the LE arm) or in the proper region (\isa{p\ {\isasymge}\ {\isadigit{1}}},
  the proper arm) from the per-tape correspondence facts rather than the
  raw cell pattern.  Both arms land back at \isa{AR{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}SimRead} on the
  successor tape with \isa{buf\ tk} holding the decoded \isa{M}-symbol
  \isa{tM\ p} (via \isa{foldl{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}acc{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}cell{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}repr}) and \isa{posk\ tk}
  re-synced exact to the head's position kind (\isa{AR{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}AtLE} at
  \isa{{\isadigit{0}}}, \isa{AR{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}AtFirstProper} at \isa{{\isadigit{1}}},
  \isa{AR{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}AtFurtherProper} beyond — the marker test
  \isa{tM{\isacharprime}{\kern0pt}\ {\isacharparenleft}{\kern0pt}base\ {\isacharminus}{\kern0pt}\ {\isadigit{1}}{\isacharparenright}{\kern0pt}\ {\isacharequal}{\kern0pt}\ LE{\isadigit{4}}} coincides with \isa{p\ {\isacharequal}{\kern0pt}\ {\isadigit{1}}} by the
  left-end discipline).  Cost \isa{{\isadigit{1}}} (LE) or \isa{b\ {\isacharplus}{\kern0pt}\ {\isadigit{2}}} (proper).
  This is the uniform per-tape step the read-phase tape walk iterates.%
\end{isamarkuptext}\isamarkuptrue%
%
\begin{isamarkuptext}%
Relation-generic read single-tape step scaffold (non-last).
  The two cases (\isa{p\ {\isacharequal}{\kern0pt}\ {\isadigit{0}}} le-step, \isa{p\ {\isasymnoteq}\ {\isadigit{0}}} proper-step)
  are the \isa{leaf{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}le} and \isa{leaf{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}proper} hypotheses; union and
  sub versions are thin instantiations.%
\end{isamarkuptext}\isamarkuptrue%

\begin{isamarkuptext}%
Unified single-tape read, last tape: as \isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}read{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}tape{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}step}
  but \isa{tk} is the last tape, so both arms transition to
  \isa{AR{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}SimCompute} with the current-tape field reset to
  \isa{k{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}unidx\ {\isadigit{0}}} (all tapes decoded, the read phase complete).  The
  \isa{buf\ tk} decode and \isa{posk\ tk} re-sync are identical to the
  non-last variant; only the hand-off target differs.%
\end{isamarkuptext}\isamarkuptrue%
%
\begin{isamarkuptext}%

  (last tape).  As \isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}read{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}tape{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}step{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}gen} but both cases use the
  last-tape finish leaves (transition to \isa{AR{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}SimCompute}); the
  le-finish and proper-finish helpers are the \isa{leaf{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}le} and
  \isa{leaf{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}proper} hypotheses.%
\end{isamarkuptext}\isamarkuptrue%

\begin{isamarkuptext}%
The read-phase prefix walk: starting from the read boundary
  (current-tape field \isa{k{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}unidx\ {\isadigit{0}}}, bit-counter
  \isa{{\isadigit{0}}}), iterate the unified non-last per-tape read
  \isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}read{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}tape{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}step} over the first \isa{j} tapes of the
  enumeration (\isa{j\ {\isasymle}\ k{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}tm\ M\ {\isacharminus}{\kern0pt}\ {\isadigit{1}}}, so every tape touched is
  non-last), landing back at \isa{AR{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}SimRead} on tape
  \isa{k{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}unidx\ j}.  The walk descriptor splits on \isa{k{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}idx\ k\ {\isacharless}{\kern0pt}\ j}: tapes already visited carry the decoded \isa{M}-symbol in
  \isa{buf}, the exact re-synced \isa{posk}, and the block-end
  head position; the rest retain their boundary values.  Variable
  per-tape cost (\isa{{\isadigit{1}}} or \isa{b\ {\isacharplus}{\kern0pt}\ {\isadigit{2}}}), aggregate bounded by
  \isa{j\ {\isasymcdot}\ {\isacharparenleft}{\kern0pt}b\ {\isacharplus}{\kern0pt}\ {\isadigit{2}}{\isacharparenright}{\kern0pt}}.  Custom induction on \isa{j}; the
  inductive step's function-update bookkeeping rests on \isa{k{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}idx}
  injectivity (\isa{k\ {\isasymnoteq}\ k{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}unidx\ j\ {\isasymLongrightarrow}\ k{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}idx\ k\ {\isasymnoteq}\ j}).%
\end{isamarkuptext}\isamarkuptrue%
%
\begin{isamarkuptext}%
Relation-generic read prefix walk scaffold.  The single
  per-tape helper (\isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}read{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}tape{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}step}) is the \isa{leaf}
  hypothesis, quantified over the per-tape config, tape index,
  buffer, position-kind, tape-content function, and position; union
  and sub versions are thin instantiations.%
\end{isamarkuptext}\isamarkuptrue%

\begin{isamarkuptext}%
The full read phase: the prefix walk over the first
  \isa{k{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}tm\ M\ {\isacharminus}{\kern0pt}\ {\isadigit{1}}} tapes followed by the unified last-tape read
  \isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}read{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}tape{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}finish{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}step}, landing at \isa{AR{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}SimCompute}
  (current-tape field \isa{k{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}unidx\ {\isadigit{0}}}) with every tape's
  \isa{M}-read symbol decoded into \isa{buf}, every \isa{posk}
  re-synced exact, and every head left at its block-end (or position
  \isa{{\isadigit{1}}} for an \isa{LE} tape).  Aggregate cost \isa{{\isasymle}\ k{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}tm\ M\ {\isasymcdot}\ {\isacharparenleft}{\kern0pt}b\ {\isacharplus}{\kern0pt}\ {\isadigit{2}}{\isacharparenright}{\kern0pt}}.  The final \isa{buf}/\isa{posk}/position
  vectors collapse from the \isa{k{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}idx\ k\ {\isacharless}{\kern0pt}\ j} split because, on the
  last tape, every other tape has index \isa{{\isacharless}{\kern0pt}\ k{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}tm\ M\ {\isacharminus}{\kern0pt}\ {\isadigit{1}}}
  (\isa{k{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}idx} bijective into \isa{{\isacharbraceleft}{\kern0pt}{\isachardot}{\kern0pt}{\isachardot}{\kern0pt}{\isacharless}{\kern0pt}\ k{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}tm\ M{\isacharbraceright}{\kern0pt}}).%
\end{isamarkuptext}\isamarkuptrue%
%
\begin{isamarkuptext}%
Relation-generic full read phase scaffold.  The two helpers
  (prefix walk, last-tape finish) are the \isa{leaf{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}prefix} and
  \isa{leaf{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}finish} hypotheses; union and sub versions are thin
  instantiations.  The finish leaf is function-heavy, so it is
  discharged via \isa{{\isacharparenleft}{\kern0pt}rule\ {\isasymdots}{\isacharsemicolon}{\kern0pt}\ assumption{\isacharparenright}{\kern0pt}} rather than positional
  \isa{OF}.%
\end{isamarkuptext}\isamarkuptrue%
